-- discord.gg/syncrypt

local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local mt = getmetatable(f)

		if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
			f, s, c = mt.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local r = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if r[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = r[1]

		return true, table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
	end
end

GetIcon = function(p1)
	if type(p1) == "string" then
		if not p1:find("rbxassetid://") then
			return "rbxassetid://" .. p1
		end
	end

	if type(p1) == "number" then
		return "rbxassetid://" .. p1
	end

	return p1
end
tw = function(p2)
	return TweenService:Create(p2.v, TweenInfo.new(p2.t, Enum.EasingStyle[p2.s], Enum.EasingDirection[p2.d]), p2.g)
end

lak = function(p6)
	local u47
	local u48

			if u51.IsDraggingDisabled() then

			UDim2.new(Position2.X.Scale, Position2.X.Offset + v53.X, Position2.Y.Scale, Position2.Y.Offset + v53.Y)

			Position = uDim2,
		}):Play()
	end

	p6.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if u51 then
			if u51.IsDraggingDisabled() then
				return
			end
		end

		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			u47 = true
			Position = input.Position
			Position2 = p6.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					u47 = false
				end
			end)

			return
		elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			u47 = true
			Position = input.Position
			Position2 = p6.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					u47 = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	p6.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if
			input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
		then
			u48 = input
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if input == u48 then
			if u47 then
				v52(input)
			end
		end
	end)

	return {
		SetResizeController = function(p8)
			u51 = p8
		end,
	}
end

EffectClick = function(p10, p11)
	local mouse = Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
	local v57 = mouse.X - p10.AbsolutePosition.X
	local v58 = mouse.Y - p10.AbsolutePosition.Y

	if v57 < 0 or v58 < 0 or p10.AbsoluteSize.X < v57 or p10.AbsoluteSize.Y < v58 then
		return
	end

	local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	Frame.Parent = p11
	Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, v57, 0, v58)
	Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	Frame.ZIndex = 10

	local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")

	UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
	UICorner.Parent = Frame

	local tween = TweenService:Create(Frame, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Size = UDim2.new(0, p10.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.5, 0, p10.AbsoluteSize.X * 1.5),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	})

	tween.Completed:Connect(function()
		Frame:Destroy()
	end)
	tween:Play()
end

local function v69(p14)
	p14:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
		local v70 = (p14.AbsoluteSize.X / 520 + p14.AbsoluteSize.Y / 340) / 2
		local v71, v72, v73 = pairs(p14:GetDescendants())
		local v74 = geniter(v71, v72, v73)
		local exitTo
		local v76

		for _, v in v71, v72, v73 do
			if v:IsA("TextLabel") or v:IsA("TextButton") or v:IsA("TextBox") then
				local UITextSizeConstraint = v:FindFirstChildOfClass("UITextSizeConstraint")

				if UITextSizeConstraint then
					UITextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = math.floor(UITextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize / v70 * v70)
				end
			end

			if
				(v.Name == "Tab" or v.Name == "Left" or v.Name == "Right" or v.Name == "SingleColumn")
				and (v:IsA("ScrollingFrame") or v:IsA("Frame"))
			then
				v76 = v
				exitTo = 1

				break
			end
		end

		if exitTo == 1 then
			local UIListLayout = v76:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout") or v76:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGridLayout")
			local v81

			while true do
				if UIListLayout then
					task.wait()
				end

				local exitTo2

				while true do
					local v83 = table.pack(v74())

					if not v83[1] then
						break
					end

					v81 = v83[3]

					if v81:IsA("TextLabel") or v81:IsA("TextButton") or v81:IsA("TextBox") then
						local UITextSizeConstraint = v81:FindFirstChildOfClass("UITextSizeConstraint")

						if UITextSizeConstraint then
							UITextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = math.floor(UITextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize / v70 * v70)
						end
					end

					if v81.Name == "Tab" or v81.Name == "Left" or v81.Name == "Right" or v81.Name == "SingleColumn" then
						if v81:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
							exitTo2 = 1

							break
						elseif v81:IsA("Frame") then
							exitTo2 = 2

							break
						end
					end
				end

				if exitTo2 == 1 then
					UIListLayout = v81:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")

					if UIListLayout then
						continue
					end

					UIListLayout = v81:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGridLayout")

					continue
				end

				if exitTo2 ~= 2 then
					break
				end

				UIListLayout = v81:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout") or v81:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGridLayout")
			end
		end
	end)
end

	local Title = p18.Title or "Title"
	local Desc = p18.Desc or "Desc"
	local IconTile = p18.IconTile or nil
	local SizeUi = p18.SizeUi or "520,340"
	local loadCustomTheme = _G.loadCustomTheme ~= "" and _G.loadCustomTheme or nil
	local t5 = {}

	for match in string.gmatch(SizeUi, "[^,]+") do
		table.insert(t5, tonumber(match))
	end

	local v158 = t5[1] or 520
	local v159 = t5[2] or 340
	local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
	local ImageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

		g = {
			Size = UDim2.new(0, v158, 0, v159),
		},
	}):Play()
	v69(Frame2)

	ImageLabel2.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
	ImageLabel2.ZIndex = 1

	if loadCustomTheme then
		local v174 = request({
			Url = loadCustomTheme,
			Method = "GET",
		})

		if v174.StatusCode == 200 then
			local match = loadCustomTheme:match("%.([^%.%?&#/]+)") or loadCustomTheme:match("%.([^%.]+)$")

			if
				not match
				or not ({
					png = true,
					jpg = true,
					jpeg = true,
					gif = true,
					bmp = true,
					webp = true,
					svg = true,
					tiff = true,
					ico = true,
					tga = true,
					dds = true,
					hdr = true,
				})[match:lower()]
			then
				local contentType = v174.Headers
					and (v174.Headers["content-type"] or v174.Headers["Content-Type"] or "")
				local t6 = {
					["image/png"] = "png",
					["image/jpeg"] = "jpg",
					["image/jpg"] = "jpg",
					["image/gif"] = "gif",

					["image/x-icon"] = "ico",
					["image/svg+xml"] = "svg",
					["image/tiff"] = "tiff",
				}

				match = t6[contentType] or t6[contentType:match("^([^;]+)")] or "png"
			end

			local v178 = "Theme." .. match:lower()

			writefile(v178, v174.Body)

			local getcustomasset_ = getcustomasset
				or getsynasset
				or function(p19)
					return "rbxasset://" .. p19
				end
			local ImageLabel3 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

			ImageLabel3.Name = "ThemeImage"
			ImageLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
